# Verifier coverage of the endpoints changed since the last release (2026-10-22)

Release gate (Andrew, 2026-10-21): every endpoint that is new or changed since the last published release is reached by a verifier lane and by a fault-injection arm the verifier or a check provably catches. Range `fc8d425fb..a54842049`. Generated by `tools/verify_coverage_audit.py` (static: the export walk of `tools/public_runtime_inventory.py`, `tools/lane_select.py`'s Mojo definition graph, and a scoped name closure from each lane's own code); the rows marked by hand are the `ANNOTATIONS` and `ANNOTATIONS_BY_SRC` tables in that file, each checked in the source.

Regenerate:

```sh
python3 tools/verify_coverage_audit.py --base fc8d425fb --json <audit.json> --markdown docs/plans/VERIFY_COVERAGE_20261022.md
```

## Summary

Native entries (one row per registration; a GPU binding and its host twin are two rows):

| status | rows |
|---|---|
| covered | 1177 |
| covered (new lane) | 32 |
| n/a | 263 |
| owed | 17 |
| GAP | 0 |
| total | 1489 |

Fault-injection arms, over the rows that are not n/a:

| arm | rows |
|---|---|
| armed | 1136 |
| n/a | 90 |
| owed | 0 |

Public Python methods whose call path changed: 342 covered 335, covered (new lane) 7.

A lane that reaches an entry `covered` is recorded unless it is one of the new lanes below, which read OWED until a release record admits them.

## New lanes (tools/identity_lanes/coverage.py)

Each ran 9 of 9 fixtures STABLE at `--repeats 2` on the Mac (Metal) build of integ/20261021; parts are Mojo FNV-1a64 digests (`verify_digest`). Declared in their family's training lanes with the pending reason `no reference`. The lanes lane verify-coverage-owed added ran with that lane's Python package over the same build.

- `classical-float16-sequences`: LinearRegression, Ridge, StandardScaler, MinMaxScaler and PCA on a list of float16 rows: the classical input door's `sequence_widen_halves` / `sequence_narrow_halves` and the scalers' `scaler_cast`
- `cross-vendor-fold`: mojolearn.cross_vendor ordered_fold and fold_pair: `fold_pair_f32`, `cross_vendor_call`
- `gp-lml-theta`: GaussianProcessRegressor.log_marginal_likelihood(theta, eval_gradient): `gp_theta_params`, `gpr_lml_grad`
- `hf-causal-lm-stream`: CausalLM.stream and models.GenerationStream.read, greedy and sampled
- `hf-causal-lm-text`: CausalLM.generate_text, stream_text and _generate_resident with a tokenizer from `Tokenizer(...)`, `_from_json` and `_from_spelled`: `causal_lm_generate_text`, `causal_lm_text_stream`, `causal_lm_resident`, `vocab_json_public`, `vocab_spelled_public`
- `hf-model-surface`: HFConfig, ModelPlan, TensorInfo, SafetensorsFile._map, Checkpoint._file and CausalLM._read_weights: `model_config_*`, `model_plan_init`, `tensor_info_*`, `safetensors_map`, `checkpoint_file`, `causal_model_read_weights`
- `ivf-short-fill`: IVFIndex.search with fewer candidates than k: the short fill on the device search and the host column (`ivf_out_of_bounds_distance`)
- `mamba2-decode-session`: Mamba2Block's resident decode session: step, sync_state and load_state (`mamba2_session_open`, `mamba2_session_export_state`, `mamba2_session_load_state`)
- `mamba3-decode-session`: Mamba3Block's resident decode session: step, sync_state and load_state (`mamba3_session_open`, `mamba3_session_step`, `mamba3_session_export_state`, `mamba3_session_load_state`)
- `safetensors-widen-f16`: float16 checkpoint weights (models.safetensors.widen_f16): `from_f16`, device and host
- `samba-optimizer-state`: SambaStack state_dict / load_state_dict on an open resident optimizer slot (`optimizer_resident_download`, `optimizer_resident_upload`) and optimizer.maximize (`optimizer_resident_step_io`)
- `saved-model-host-infer-trees`: HostForest and host_predict on saved Extra Trees and default-engine random forest regressors: `forest_host_et_predict`, `forest_host_rf_predict_reg`
- `sequence-device-tensor`: resident sequence tensors (`seq_tensor_alloc`, `seq_tensor_upload`, `seq_tensor_download`, `seq_tensor_copy`) through RMSprop
- `sequence-export-roundtrip`: Sequential.export, Sequential.load, predict and predict_proba
- `sequence-layernorm-backward`: the functional layer_norm_backward
- `sequence-moe-backward`: MoEBlock.backward: `moe_backward`
- `sequence-onnx-import`: mojolearn.import_model on an ONNX file: `onnx_import`, the Mojo ONNX reader
- `sequence-optimizer-state`: NAdam and RMSprop state_dict / load_state_dict: `seq_optimizer_export`, `seq_optimizer_import`
- `sequence-program-save-load`: ops.Program.save and Session.load_program (the public `ops.Program` class)
- `training-chunked-lm-head`: training.chunked_lm_head_loss with and without gradients: `chunked_lm_head_v2_loss`, `chunked_lm_head_v2_train`
- `training-device-cross-entropy`: training.cross_entropy, linear_forward and linear_backward on device arrays (GPU columns): `ce_loss_dev`, `linear_forward_dev`, `linear_backward_dev`
- `training-mlp-sessions`: training.mlp_inference_sessions: `mlp_sessions`
- `transformer-decode-load-state`: TransformerDecodeSession.load_state: `transformer_decode_session_load_state`
- `x-decomp-rsvd-rank-deficient`: randomized_svd, PCA and TruncatedSVD (randomized) of a rank-deficient matrix: the live-column compaction (`x_decomp_order_small`, `x_decomp_dev_order_small`)
- `x-huber-outliers`: HuberRegressor.outliers_ (`py2mojo_rows` ROWS_HUBER_OUT)
- `x-logistic-cv-proba`: LogisticRegressionCV.predict_proba (`py2mojo_rows` ROWS_LRCV_PROBA)
- `x-metrics-classification-report`: metrics.classification_report, dict and text
- `x-metrics-permutation-test-score`: model_selection.permutation_test_score, with and without groups
- `x-sgd-clf-proba`: SGDClassifier.predict_proba (`py2mojo_rows` ROWS_SGD_PROBA)

## Fault-injection arms added (tools/identity_lanes/coverage_sabotage.tsv)

One source patch per changed kernel file that no arm reached, each a value fault in the changed definitions (a float one ulp off, an index permuted, a byte flipped), applied only to a scratch copy. `python3 tools/coverage_sabotage.py plan --arm <arm>` describes clean, sabotaged and restored runs. `check` requires matching source/device/input metadata, complete fixtures, valid clean results, fault detection in every named lane, and restoration to clean output. Refusals require an exact fault-specific review; missing bindings and other infrastructure failures do not count. Retained execution scope is listed under Arms proven below; a declared arm is not proof it ran.

## Arms proven (tools/identity_lanes/coverage_sabotage_proven.tsv)

Run one at a time on the Mac (lane run-sabotage-arms, 2026-10-21) over the merged Metal (identical) and host builds of 51bf10f4f, whose kernel sources equal 79982adaa's. Per arm: the patch applied, the named per-binding builds rebuilt (a binding's own file wins over the merged one), the arm's lanes run (`identity_break.py --fixtures base --repeats 1`) on Metal and on the host column (`MOJOLEARN_VENDOR=cpu`) for the columns whose bindings were rebuilt, then the patch reverted and its builds removed and the lanes run again. A lane is caught when a column it runs on MOVED or REFUSED against the clean run of the same lanes; the sabotaged cells are also judged against `python/mojolearn/verify_reference/table.json` (OWED: a new lane with no reference yet, judged against the clean run only; IDENTICAL: a column the fault does not reach while the other column moved). Every REFUSED cell is the fault tripping one of the library's own checks.

| arm | builds (rc, seconds) | verdict | lanes (column: result) | sabotaged cells against table.json | patch reverted | seconds |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `coverage-core-array_helpers_impl` | build rc 0, 87s, build_core_host rc 0, 23s | CAUGHT | x-metrics-search (host MOVED Metal MOVED) trees-adaboost-clf (host MOVED Metal MOVED) | 4 DIVERGENT | STABLE, equal to clean | 147 |
| `coverage-core-hotpath_impl` | build rc 0, 110s, build_core_host rc 0, 38s | CAUGHT | x-metrics-splitters (host MOVED Metal MOVED) cross-val (host MOVED Metal unmoved) x-lasso-cv (host MOVED Metal unmoved) | 4 DIVERGENT, 2 IDENTICAL | STABLE, equal to clean | 190 |
| `coverage-core-hotpath_impl_rows` | build rc 0, 23s, build_core_host rc 0, 31s | CAUGHT | x-metrics-splitters (host REFUSED Metal MOVED) sequence-mlp (host MOVED Metal unmoved) x-prep-user-objects (host REFUSED Metal REFUSED) | 2 DIVERGENT, 1 IDENTICAL, 3 REFUSED | STABLE, equal to clean | 87 |
| `coverage-core-host_helpers_impl` | build_core_host rc 0, 27s, build_forest_host rc 0, 10s | CAUGHT | rf-clf (host MOVED) et-clf (host MOVED) sequence-autoarima (host MOVED) | 3 DIVERGENT | STABLE, equal to clean | 52 |
| `coverage-core-core_host_impl` | build_core_host rc 0, 38s | CAUGHT | elasticnet (host MOVED) spectral-precomputed (host MOVED) | 2 DIVERGENT | STABLE, equal to clean | 59 |
| `coverage-core-cast_in` | build_x_sequence rc 0, 207s, build_x_sequence_host rc 0, 104s | CAUGHT | sequence-export-roundtrip (host MOVED Metal MOVED) | 2 OWED (no reference) | STABLE, equal to clean | 336 |
| `coverage-core-label_encode` | build rc 0, 70s, build_core_host rc 0, 20s, build_x_sequence rc 0, 128s, build_x_sequence_host rc 0, 100s | CAUGHT | sequence-rnn (host MOVED Metal MOVED) sequence-tape-sequential (host MOVED Metal MOVED) sequence-export-roundtrip (host MOVED Metal MOVED) | 2 DIVERGENT, 4 OWED (no reference) | STABLE, equal to clean | 341 |

| `coverage-core-mojolearn_rf_host_impl` | build_rf_host rc 0, 13s | CAUGHT | rf-clf (host MOVED) rf-reg (host MOVED) | 2 DIVERGENT | STABLE, equal to clean | 25 |
| `coverage-core-mojolearn_rf_impl` | build_rf rc 0, 53s | CAUGHT | rf-clf (Metal MOVED) rf-reg (Metal MOVED) | 2 DIVERGENT | STABLE, equal to clean | 65 |

55 of 55 arms caught; 9069 arm seconds in all.

Fixed during the run (no lane weakened; every arm whose patch or lanes gained something was rerun and caught; the arms that only stopped naming an unreachable lane keep their run):

- `coverage-core-hotpath_impl_rows`: x-prep-user-objects did not move. The patch swapped fold labels 0 and 1 in both `assign_fold_i64` and `count_fold_hits_i64`, a consistent relabeling no output can show; the swap now stays in `assign_fold_i64` only, so TargetEncoder's `cv=<splitter>` check refuses. The patch also faults the new host `bincount2_i32`.
- `coverage-x_decomp-select_ops`: x-decomp-lstsq-rsvd never reaches the live-column compaction (its sketches are no wider than the rank), so it did not move. New lane `x-decomp-rsvd-rank-deficient` (a rank-4 matrix with an 8-wide sketch; 9 of 9 fixtures STABLE, Metal equal to the host column) catches it, and `coverage-x_decomp-select_dev` names it too.
- Lanes the arm's fault cannot reach, no longer named: par-cholesky and par-hdbscan on the host-binding arms (`coverage-decomposition-linalg_host_impl`, `coverage-hdbscan-hdbscan_door_host_impl`; both read N/A on the host column, which has no multi-device driver), trees-adaboost-reg on `coverage-core-array_helpers_impl` (AdaBoost.R2 predicts by the weighted median and calls none of the faulted helpers).
- A real host-column bug: `StratifiedGroupKFold.split` refused on a CPU-only install because the GPU base binding's `bincount2_i32` and `strat_group_plan_i32` had no host twin. The core host binding has both now; x-metrics-splitters on the host column is IDENTICAL to the reference on 9 of 9 fixtures, and `coverage-core-hotpath_impl` and `coverage-core-hotpath_impl_rows` were rerun with the fix in the clean set.
- `coverage-tokenizer-ranks` also lists `build_tokenizer_fast`: that is the FAST-tier binding, which the verifier refuses, so it was not built; the arm is caught through the host tokenizer.

## Owed

- `seq_tensor_free`: New GPU-only source route: native sequence finalization and failed upload/copy cleanup -> retained seq_tensor_free. The new lane independently checks pool closure after explicit, repeated, GC and forced native-operation failures; a no-op free fault is authored. CPU has no resident pool. Bounded Metal clean execution passed on native43 with harness7f64. Fault/restoration, all-fixture reference and installed-package admission remain pending.
- `optimizer_door_io`: New source route: native public optimizer io/raw_io/param_device -> retained optimizer_door_io. The named new lanes consume the actual bare owner operation. CPU IO scope is query and refusal only; optim-resident-io qualifies GPU transfers. Independent new sabotage arms are authored. Bounded CPU/Metal clean runs passed on native43 with harness7f64; fault/restoration, all-fixture reference and installed-package admission remain pending.
- `optimizer_door_load_state`: New source route: native public optimizer load -> retained optimizer_door_load_state. The named new lanes consume the actual bare owner operation. CPU IO scope is query and refusal only; optim-resident-io qualifies GPU transfers. Independent new sabotage arms are authored. Bounded CPU/Metal clean runs passed on native43 with harness7f64; fault/restoration, all-fixture reference and installed-package admission remain pending.
- `optimizer_door_state_set`: New source route: native public optimizer state_set -> retained optimizer_door_state_set. The named new lanes consume the actual bare owner operation. CPU IO scope is query and refusal only; optim-resident-io qualifies GPU transfers. Independent new sabotage arms are authored. Bounded CPU/Metal clean runs passed on native43 with harness7f64; fault/restoration, all-fixture reference and installed-package admission remain pending.
- `optimizer_door_zero`: New source route: native public optimizer zero -> retained optimizer_door_zero. The named new lanes consume the actual bare owner operation. CPU IO scope is query and refusal only; optim-resident-io qualifies GPU transfers. Independent new sabotage arms are authored. Bounded CPU/Metal clean runs passed on native43 with harness7f64; fault/restoration, all-fixture reference and installed-package admission remain pending.
- `lr_schedule_series`: New source route: schedule lr_values/lrs -> native schedule_values/schedule_list -> retained lr_schedule_series. The new lane consumes both outputs over phase boundaries and empty series; coverage-native-schedule-series faults returned values. Bounded CPU/Metal clean runs passed on native43 with harness7f64. Fault/restoration records, all-fixture cross-vendor references and installed-package admission remain pending.
- `robust_scaler_native_call`: Public RobustScaler lifecycle calls robust_lifecycle_c, which prepares native buffers and runs the existing x_prep quantile/scale/affine stages. Existing lanes consume center_, scale_ and transformed outputs; new public lifecycle/refusal tests are authored. New source only: current runtime execution, fault/restoration, installed-wheel and matching reference admission remain pending.
- `minmax_transform`: Source reach requires MOJOLEARN_IDENTITY_HOST_INFER=minmax-scaler: the lane saves its scaler, host_model loads HostMinMaxScaler, and the held-out transform uses the estimators_host entry. Ordinary CPU records exercise preprocessing_host instead. Bounded local CPU host-infer execution passed at e7c23510b (base fixture, one repeat; retained scaler-host-infer-cpu-e7c23510b.result.json). Matching installed-package and reference qualification are still owed; this is not full fixture coverage.
- `standard_transform`: Source reach requires MOJOLEARN_IDENTITY_HOST_INFER=standard-scaler: the lane saves its scaler, host_model loads HostStandardScaler, and the held-out transform uses the estimators_host entry. Ordinary CPU records exercise preprocessing_host instead. Bounded local CPU host-infer execution passed at e7c23510b (base fixture, one repeat; retained scaler-host-infer-cpu-e7c23510b.result.json). Matching installed-package and reference qualification are still owed; this is not full fixture coverage.
- `host_svc_attribute_call`, `host_svr_attribute_call`, `optimizer_device_finalize`: Targeted public operation and ownership tests are registered; retain their exact frozen execution scope separately. Matching installed-package, reference and fault/restoration admission for this entry remains pending; static lane reachability does not establish it.

Sabotage arms: none.

## Method and limits

- A registration is CHANGED when a Mojo definition its export reaches (lane_select's definition graph, by name through imports) differs from the base, moves excluded (the same text at the base under the same name in any file). A shared helper's change therefore marks every export that reaches it.
- A lane REACHES an entry when the entry's name is in the lane's scoped closure: the lane function, its helpers and property probes (the harness's shared helpers excluded), the package definitions those name (resolved through imports, methods of reached classes by attribute name, import-time calls, module tables, `name + "_suffix"` composition), or the public method walk of tools/public_runtime_inventory.py. It is static: a reach is a call path, not a run. New and existing lanes require separately retained execution, reference and installed-package records for each claimed target and option; this table does not admit them.
- A row is ARMED when a definition in its export's closure reads a `MOJOLEARN_*SABOTAGE*` define or is edited by a `*/sabotage/*.patch`. `CPU gate HOST_SABOTAGE` is the arm the CPU identity gate builds and requires to move every covered part; family defines and seam patches are their checks' arms; coverage arms are this audit's (run: Arms proven). Definitions with no value to fault are listed with the reason in tools/identity_lanes/coverage_sabotage_na.tsv.
- `n/a` lanes: no Python caller in the package, the fast tier (the verifier refuses fast), a verifier or build probe, or a dead helper; each with its reason in the row.
